# 52 — Translation ledger, every upstream item against its Zig counterpart

**What this document is.** One row per public or `pub(crate)` item in the
pinned upstream `accesskit` and `accesskit-c` trees, with the upstream
`path:line`, the upstream name, the kind, the Zig `path:line`, the Zig
name, and a port decision. `AGENTS.md` section 6 requires this table.

**Immutable inputs.** Core and adapter paths refer to `AccessKit/accesskit`
at `466e24e252103f4d82bd6fbab98141b551e46e0c`.
C paths refer to `AccessKit/accesskit-c` at `8b6ed37c20ed4c59390e253407983333053662ba`.
The old directory labels `accesskit-main` and `accesskit-c-main` below do not identify moving branches.
The original 1238-row inventory grew to 1356 rows.
Item dispositions record source correspondence, not native execution.

The initial documentation source baseline was `101f912320e225626ca833626dc2895eb0343418`.
Later owner source snapshots are explicit below.
An owner snapshot is not a final integration revision or a build receipt.
Current formatted counterpart coordinates use immutable `d854c8b0d77385cf8c0c5983832369923fdef2d5`.
Earlier actual complete-graph receipts belong to pre-format source `3b4e07e5d05a960d57095405c339e7787436014a`.
Main's complete root graph at current source `d854c8b0d77385cf8c0c5983832369923fdef2d5` passed 2027 tests with 43 SDK skips, zero failures, total 2070 in each of Debug and ReleaseSafe; 31/31 build steps succeeded (`artifact://2946`). This is root-graph success, not a green project gate.
The earlier source320 receipt passed 2028 tests with 43 skips, zero failures, total 2071 in each mode (`artifact://2827`). It remains evidence for immutable `320cb011918e099317136c226d43a2abf3d665d0`, not for later source.
Those pre-format source3b4 complete-graph Debug and ReleaseSafe receipts each record 2008 passed, 43 skipped, 0 failed, total 2051.
The final gate is separate and remains pending. Forty-three skips are not native SDK passes.

**The four decisions.**

|Decision|What it means|Rows|
|---|---|---|
|ported|the Zig item has the same source job|433|
|adapted|the Zig item has a different shape, with a reason in the row|740|
|not-applicable|the upstream item needs no exact counterpart, with a reason in the row|183|
|open|source or named acceptance remains incomplete, even if a counterpart exists|0|

**The original inventory remains 1356 rows.** The final dispositions above count those rows only.
This reconciliation replaces 280 original mappings and adds 118 separate supplemental source mappings.
The supplemental tables contain 31 public port additions, 12 core operations, and five external wire-domain mappings.
They also contain 54 Mac selectors, 13 Mac text selectors, and three C optional-scalar mappings.
These are data-inventory counts, not build, test, or execution results.
`local://execution-docs-final-ledger-measured.json` recounts the literal original table, excluding all supplemental tables.

All 57 former open rows now have exact source mappings or an explicit unresolved reason.
No original row remains open. Unix `bus.rs:39` has controlled real-native acceptance; Android `event.rs:235` has complete portable namespace acceptance.

status epochs, native signals, and bounded provider close/join.

The sender correction is an existing-port fix, not a new public API or inventory row.
The exact exception is `org.freedesktop.DBus`; arbitrary well-known senders remain rejected.
Upstream: `zbus_names` 4.3.4 `src/unique_name.rs:31-39`
([source](https://docs.rs/crate/zbus_names/4.3.4/source/src/unique_name.rs#L31-L39)),
and D-Bus 0.43 [SENDER header rules](https://dbus.freedesktop.org/doc/dbus-specification.html#message-protocol-header-fields)
and [reserved message-bus identity](https://dbus.freedesktop.org/doc/dbus-specification.html#message-bus).
Zig: `src/adapters/atspi/dbus.zig:512-518,549,855,1294-1323,1325-1340,1341-1357`.
The fifth corpus descriptor identifies the actual 89-byte daemon Hello return,
`src/adapters/atspi/dbus_corpus/daemon-hello-return.bin`,
SHA-256 `59de2c6f17737b660704386754a904f823fd2e5543c254277190fb12c59e39d7`.
Its source capture is `execute-1-4-green/native-startup-bounded-wire-red.json`.
Codec receipts are distinct from live peer proof; see `docs/50-verification.md` section 11.

**Duplicated upstream locations are expected.** The C ABI generates 353 of its
496 exported functions from `macro_rules!` invocations in
`accesskit-c/src/common.rs`, so many rows share one `common.rs:NNN` line.

The three operations survive as `accesskit_*_free` and the `*_new` functions, all of which are rows in this ledger|
|`accesskit-c-main/src/lib.rs:136`|`ref_from_ptr`|pub(crate) fn|`src/capi/marshal.zig:1`|`per-module conversion functions`|adapted (upstream is one generic function shared by every handle, with the return lifetime chosen by the caller. Zig has neither generic lifetime elision nor a shared generic pointer cast, so the port splits it: each C ABI module takes its own typed pointer parameter, and the cast is written at that module's boundary. There is no single counterpart function, which is why this row points at the layer rather than a line|
|`accesskit-c-main/src/lib.rs:144`|`mut_from_ptr`|pub(crate) fn|`src/capi/marshal.zig:1`|`per-module conversion functions`|adapted (as `ref_from_ptr`: the mutable form is likewise written at each module's boundary rather than shared)|

The allocation layer releases its own blocks through the declared allocator. Typed C handles use their per-handle release functions and captured allocators. The port has no shared Box conversion)|
|`accesskit-c-main/src/lib.rs:206`|`debug_repr_from_ptr`|pub(crate) fn|`src/capi/tree_info.zig:399`|`treeInfoDebug`|adapted (upstream is one generic function that formats any `Debug` value and hands the `CString` over. Zig has no `Debug` bound, so the port writes one formatter per type and three call sites: `src/capi/tree_info.zig:399`, `src/capi/tree_update.zig` for the update, and the node and adapter debug exports. All three say so in their own doc comments, citing this function by line|

|`accesskit-c-main/src/lib.rs:83`|`CastPtr`|pub(crate) trait|`src/capi/handles.zig:39`|`opaque {} handle types`|not-applicable (the trait's whole job is to record which Rust type a snake_case C handle maps to, so that casts can be written without `as` and without losing const-ness. Zig has no implicit pointer casts to police, so there is no such record to keep; each C ABI module does its own conversion through `src/capi/marshal.zig` and the handle is declared `opaque {}` at `src/capi/handles.zig`, which the module comment there states is what stops a C consumer reading through the pointer)|
|`accesskit-c-main/src/lib.rs:93`|`CastConstPtr`|pub(crate) trait|`src/capi/handles.zig:39`|`opaque {} handle types`|not-applicable (as `CastPtr`: it exists to stop an `Arc` being cast to a mutable pointer. |`accesskit-main/accesskit_consumer/src/text.rs:595`|`RangePropertyValue`|enum|`src/consumer/text/range.zig:75`|`RangePropertyValue`|adapted (a generic function returning a union(enum) at comptime, where upstream is a generic enum, and the Zig single tag holds ?T so the Option that upstream puts in the type parameter is inside the tag)|
|`accesskit-main/accesskit_consumer/src/text.rs:601`|`map`|fn|`src/consumer/text/range.zig:105`|`map`|adapted (a free function whose first parameter is the value, not a method: every other operation in consumer/text/range.zig is a free function, per the module doc at range.zig:9-19. The answer is RangePropertyValue(U) and not RangePropertyValue(?U), because the port's RangePropertyValue(T) is upstream's RangePropertyValue<Option<T>>: the ledger row for text.rs:595 records that the Option sits in the tag where upstream puts it in the type parameter, so upstream's impl block on RangePropertyValue<Option<T>> returning RangePropertyValue<Option<U>> is the port's impl on RangePropertyValue(T) returning RangePropertyValue(U). Upstream's f: impl FnOnce(T) -> U becomes a *const fn (T) U, because a Zig closure would carry state that upstream's parameter does not. Three tests at range.zig:1336, :1342 and :1346 pin the three answers text.rs:605-607 gives: a present value maps, mixed passes through untouched, and a single that holds no value stays single and empty because upstream's Option::map skips the call on None. docs/10-accesskit-core.md:2103 declares the method form pub fn map(self: RangePropertyValue(?T), f: *const fn (T) U) RangePropertyValue(?U); that row is a survey and it is superseded here, the same way the ledger adapts the other eleven text.rs operations from methods to free functions)|

## Supplemental source mappings, outside the original inventory

These tables do not add upstream-item rows to the 1356-row inventory.
Public port additions have no exact upstream API equivalent.
Their motivations name upstream behavior, not an invented upstream declaration.
All coordinates identify source, not executed acceptance.

### Public port additions

|Port addition|Upstream API disposition|Exact Zig location|Motivation and contract|
|---|---|---|---|
|`Tree.initBorrowed`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/consumer/tree.zig:307`|Borrowed constructor keeps source nodes and array. Motivation: upstream accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:592-618 constructor algorithm|
|`Tree.updateBorrowedAndProcessChanges`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/consumer/tree.zig:430`|Borrowed transaction keeps caller ownership. Motivation: upstream accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:620-628 update algorithm|
|`applyUpdateBorrowed`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/consumer/tree_apply.zig:122`|Borrows headers and payloads on private working state. Motivation: upstream accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:124-330 apply algorithm|
|`WindowsAdapter.UpdateOutcome`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/adapter.zig:67`|Checked updates distinguish publication and event preparation. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/adapter.rs:463 returns Option<QueuedEvents>|
|`WindowsAdapter.BorrowedUpdateOptions and checked updates`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/adapter.zig:81,221,242`|Explicit owned and borrowed update contracts replace implicit Rust ownership. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/adapter.rs:463|
|`Windows ActionSink and ActivationSource`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/adapter.zig:40,49,53,60`|Tagged native borrowed and C-owned callbacks prevent ownership ambiguity. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/adapter.rs:534 activation|
|`Windows ProviderFactory and RuntimeRelease`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/context.zig:43,54`|Injected provider factory and final-control-block release authority. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/context.rs:33 Arc ownership|
|`Windows WeakContext and ContextLease`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/context.zig:61,197`|Explicit weak handle and guarded lease protect adapter/tree access. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/context.rs:68 and node.rs:805|
|`Windows QueuedEvents raise and cleanup reports`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/queued_events.zig:51-74`|Retryable cleanup does not repeat event delivery. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/adapter.rs:643 raise|
|`Windows nativeFactory and WindowsBoundary`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/windows/root.zig:48,57-61`|Exposes injected native provider constructors and boundary types. Motivation: upstream adapters/windows/src/node.rs:805 and text.rs:210|
|`boundary.createActionRequest`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/capi/boundary.zig:52-71`|Allocator-first conversion gives the callback an independent C request. Motivation: accesskit-c/src/common.rs:1169-1195 transfers request ownership|
|`Installed C allocator over-alignment adapter`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/capi/allocation.zig:440-510`|Stores the original raw pointer for higher alignment. Installed callbacks need only eight-byte alignment. Motivation: C allocator ownership, no Rust allocator API counterpart|
|`operation_failed and exact C error name`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/capi/allocation.zig:69-81,214-220,239-288`|Preserves finite original errors instead of labeling every failure OutOfMemory. Category values zero and one remain unchanged|
|`accesskit_last_error_name and ACCESSKIT_ERROR_OPERATION_FAILED`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/capi/root.zig:148; include/zaccesskit.h:1780-1784,6507-6515`|Public C diagnostics add static nullable error names and category two. The original C API has no diagnostic counterpart|
|`Positioned EditableText callback`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/objects.zig:36-48,83`|Optional immutable sink preserves pinned NotSupported when absent. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/atspi/interfaces/editable_text.rs:27-52|
|`Positioned EditableText producer and capture`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/interfaces/editable_text.zig:31-81; src/adapters/atspi/dispatch.zig:43-46,75-86,157`|Scalar offsets and ranges accompany a borrowed UTF-8 byte prefix. The callback borrows the received frame synchronously and preserves exact errors|
|`Mac ActivationSink and Options`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/macos/native.zig:31-53`|Native borrowed action and C-owned activation contracts have explicit callback authority. Motivation: upstream adapters/macos/src/context.rs:16-23|
|`Mac FullTreeSupplier`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/macos/native.zig:59`|Same-frame full snapshot loan does not allocate or recompute. Motivation: upstream adapters/macos/src/adapter.rs:114 update activation|
|`Mac checked borrowed updates`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/macos/native.zig:436,452`|Borrowed synchronous or prepared notification transactions keep caller inputs. Motivation: upstream adapters/macos/src/adapter.rs:114|
|`Mac UpdateOutcome and PreparedUpdateOutcome`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/macos/native.zig:65,76; src/adapters/macos/root.zig:65-66`|Explicit rejected and committed states separate tree publication from notification failure. Motivation: upstream adapters/macos/src/adapter.rs:114 Option<QueuedEvents>|
|`Mac RuntimeRelease`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/macos/context.zig:16,65; src/adapters/macos/root.zig:67`|Success-only runtime authority lasts through final weak disposal. Motivation: upstream adapters/macos/src/context.rs:34 shared context ownership|
|`Mac Context.takeSelectorError`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/macos/context.zig:90-96,77-85; src/adapters/macos/platform_node.zig:190-199`|Consumes the first unexpected selector failure under the gate. Closed and NoSuchNode are normal sentinels; successful retry does not erase a pending cause. No allocation or callback shutdown occurs. After close, the owner or a retained weak anchor must keep Context alive. Motivation: pinned adapters/macos/src/node.rs:396-1367 selector module, not a Rust diagnostic accessor|
|`Public adapter and Runtime facade`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/root.zig:60,64; src/adapters/root.zig:6-10`|Seven root names include adapters and Runtime alongside schema, consumer, capi and version constants. Platform namespaces remain atspi, windows, macos, android and ios|
|`Linux UpdateOutcome and BorrowedUpdateOptions`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/adapter.zig:36,69,314; src/adapters/atspi/root.zig:39-40`|Checked borrowed publication distinguishes inactive, rejected and committed. Notification failure does not erase a commit. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/adapter.rs:196-219|
|`Linux UpdateTicket and UpdateContext`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/adapter.zig:51,60; src/adapters/atspi/root.zig:41-42`|Attachment epochs invalidate stale prepared deltas. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/context.rs:83-139,158-166 per-attachment activation|
|`Linux FactoryUpdate and UpdateFactory`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/adapter.zig:78,86; src/adapters/atspi/root.zig:43-44`|Factory distinguishes full and delta input with explicit ownership. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/adapter.rs:196-219 pending full tree contract|
|`Linux ActivationSnapshot`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/adapter.zig:97; src/adapters/atspi/root.zig:37`|Scoped full snapshot loan releases exactly once before activation completion. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/context.rs:83-139 activation construction|
|`Linux optional activation_completed callback`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/adapter.zig:116`|Null skips the result notification. Baseline tracking uses only committed outcomes. Motivation: port-specific publication outcome, no Rust completion callback|
|`Linux optional editable_text callback`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/adapter.zig:123,210-212,870-888`|Owner-lane callback borrows request and replacement through return. Null preserves upstream NotSupported. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/atspi/interfaces/editable_text.rs:34-51|
|`Linux Pump lifecycle and bounded channels`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/atspi/pump.zig:24-25,30-34,40-49,97-122,126-164,198-232,235-268,270-289,291-317,335-350,501-556,558-588,591-651,652-683,708-726; src/adapters/atspi/bus.zig:634-655,699-729,933-953`|Per-owner lifecycle and bounded channels replace global bootstrap. Options.pump_steps_limit defaults to u64 max and budgets owner, reader, and writer dispatch separately. Zero stops loops, not native startup I/O. PumpLimit stops writer admission, settles queued work not performed with that exact cause, and preserves an in-progress write's real result. Address alternatives use the input-byte bound and retain the last candidate error. Motivation: upstream adapters/unix/src/context.rs:33-76,169-313; no Rust Pump API or retry implementation|
|`Android callObjectNullableChecked`|No exact upstream equivalent|`src/adapters/android/java_call.zig:156-168; src/adapters/android/queued_events.zig:341; src/adapters/android/root.zig:549`|Null is a normal object result; lookup failure remains JavaMemberMissing and JNI exception remains JavaException. View.getParent null skips detached-host dispatch. Error cleanup releases a produced local reference. Motivation: pinned AccessKit adapters/android/src/event.rs:58-62, not a Rust helper API|

### Changed core implementation operations

These operations explain the ownership and atomic publication adaptation of existing upstream rows.
They are not additional public upstream items.

|Upstream behavior|Exact Zig location|Operation|Source adaptation|
|---|---|---|---|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:592-618`|`src/consumer/tree.zig:315`|`Tree.initImpl`|Root-first/no-root/nonroot guards shared by both constructors.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:620-628`|`src/consumer/tree.zig:438`|`Tree.updateImpl`|Dirty recovery from committed state before another application; success clears flag.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:640-706`|`src/consumer/tree.zig:481`|`Tree.processChanges`|Fallible prepare precedes callbacks; void emission and commit follow.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:640-706`|`src/consumer/tree.zig:492`|`Tree.prepareCommit`|Recorded Zig atomicity deviation: validate all lookups, own changed payload copies, privately reserve registry growth/maps before publication.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:640-677`|`src/consumer/tree.zig:606`|`Tree.emitChanges`|Existing ordered old/new callbacks, infallible after preflight.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:679-706`|`src/consumer/tree.zig:658`|`Tree.commitPrepared`|Owner transfer, assume-capacity insertion, ordered removal and scalar publication only; no returnable error/allocation.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:229-315`|`src/consumer/tree_apply.zig:208`|`existing-root parent rewrite`|Record parent-only promotion even when node data compares equal.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:229-315`|`src/consumer/tree_apply.zig:283`|`addNode`|Clone cleanup guard ends at successful owner insertion, not later change-record allocation.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:470-481`|`src/consumer/tree_grafts.zig:245`|`GraftScratch.resolve`|Reserve existing non-added root's updated-ID slot independently of pending graft count before logical mutations.|
|`accesskit_consumer/src/tree.rs:461-465,474-478`|`src/consumer/tree_grafts.zig:481`|`setRootParent`|Setter unchanged; its assume-capacity insertion is now covered for metadata-only root attachment.|
|`accesskit/src/lib.rs:1046`|`src/schema/props/table.zig:678`|`propertyValueCloneInto`|Custom-action capacity preflight and description-owner transfer before old-owner teardown.|
|`accesskit/src/lib.rs:1012`|`src/schema/props/table.zig:866`|`cloneActions`|Rollback destroys prior cloned descriptions, not only the vector header.|
